=== playtest v0: interactive player<->animation<->camera<->collision demo ===
CONTROLS : W/A/S/D move (real moveSlot/composeVelocity, camera-relative via REAL
           CameraSystem::movementRows()); hold Left Shift to sprint; mouse look
           (real CameraSystem rotate()/elevate()->cameraHook->preUpdate->yawStep/
           pitchStep seam); walk up to the parked car and press E to enter/exit it
           (W/S throttle/brake, A/D steer while driving - unless --no-vehicle);
           T skips the TOD clock forward 3 game hour(s) per press (CHOSEN manual
           fast-forward, v0.3 polish task - a full in-game day now takes 24 REAL
           minutes, too slow to just wait out during a play session); press R to
           arm/disarm the police roadblock spawn (debug gate, OFF by default - see
           this command's own doc comment's "THE HONEST NOTORIETY GATE" note); Esc
           or close the window to quit.
NOT IN V0: jump, crouch, gamepad/XInput (spec-tables-ui-controls.md documents the
           real gamepad binding layer; not wired here - keyboard+mouse only, said
           honestly rather than faking a controller path), ambient PEDESTRIAN
           population (the distant/LOD human tier - no spec-confirmed pass wiring
           it into playtest yet) - see this command's own doc comment above
           runPlaytest() for the full breakdown. Vehicle entry/driving IS wired (ON
           by default, --no-vehicle opts out), WITH real world collision (spec-
           physics-runtime.md Sec10a, vehicle-world-collision follow-up task -
           superseding this line's own former "no world collision" wording) - see
           that same doc comment's own honest scope note (a CHOSEN simple chase
           camera, one PLAYER vehicle only, no vehicle-vs-vehicle collision/damage).
           Near-field AMBIENT VEHICLE TRAFFIC IS wired (ALWAYS on, no flag) and the
           POLICE ROADBLOCK spawn IS wired (debug key 'R', OFF by default) - see
           this command's own doc comment's 2026-10-09 update for the full CHOSEN
           breakdown of both. The health/ammo/money/notoriety HUD overlay IS wired
           (--hud, opt-in, off unless passed) - see that flag's own honest caveat in
           this command's doc comment. The top-right corner minimap (v0.3 polish
           task) is now part of that SAME --hud overlay - real live player/vehicle
           position and heading feed updateMinimapLiveTransform(), flat per-tile
           fallback colour only (no real minimap_CCRR texture lookup wired into this
           command).
